## Estimate

Step share is a roofline estimate from edge shapes and formats, per node: max(bytes / 249.0 GB/s, FLOPs / peak), with peaks BF16 123.7, FP8 243.6 and NVFP4 490.8 TFLOPS (the MMA class of the node's input activation). State traffic: the paged KV cache over 4096 tokens per sequence, and FP32 recurrent states read and written once per sequence. Routed experts read E(1 - (1 - k/E)^T) distinct experts for T tokens. Launch overhead and conv windows are not counted. Replace with measured profiles once a serve runs.
